\displaystyle\text{(i)}\leq\mathbb{E}\biggl[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\epsilon_{i}|\biggr]\cdot\sup_{f_{j}\in\mathcal{F}_{j}}\max_{i\in[n]}\bigl|f_{j}(X_{i})-\widehat{f}^{M}[f_{j,i}]\bigr|\leq n\cdot\varepsilon_{M}.

(F.2)

Term (ii).
Let
f_{j,\mathcal{D}_{n}}=(f_{j}(X_{i}))_{i\in[n]}\in\mathbb{R}^{1\times n}
. We have

\displaystyle\sup_{f_{j}\in\mathcal{F}_{j}}\bigl\|\widehat{f}^{m}[f_{j}]-\widehat{f}^{m+1}[f_{j}]\bigr\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{f_{j}\in\mathcal{F}_{j}}\bigl\|\widehat{f}^{m}[f_{j}]-f_{j,\mathcal{D}_{n}}\bigr\|_{2}+\sup_{f_{j}\in\mathcal{F}_{j}}\bigl\|f_{j,\mathcal{D}_{n}}-\widehat{f}^{m+1}[f_{j}]\bigr\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{n}\cdot\sup_{f_{j}\in\mathcal{F}_{j}}\bigl\|\widehat{f}^{m}[f_{j}]-f_{j,\mathcal{D}_{n}}\bigr\|_{\infty}+\sqrt{n}\cdot\sup_{f_{j}\in\mathcal{F}_{j}}\bigl\|f_{j,\mathcal{D}_{n}}-\widehat{f}^{m+1}[f_{j}]\bigr\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{n}\cdot\varepsilon_{m}+\sqrt{n}\cdot\varepsilon_{m+1}=3\sqrt{n}\cdot\varepsilon_{m+1}.

(F.3)

Combining (
F.1
) with the Massart’s finite class lemma
(
Mohri et al. 2018
)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\biggl[\sup_{f_{j}\in\mathcal{F}_{j}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}\cdot\bigl(\widehat{f}^{m}[f_{j,i}]-\widehat{f}^{m+1}[f_{j,i}]\bigr)\biggr]

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{n}\cdot\varepsilon_{m+1}\cdot\sqrt{2\log\bigl(|\mathfrak{N}_{m}|\cdot|\mathfrak{N}_{m+1}|\bigr)}

\displaystyle\leq 6\sqrt{n}\cdot\varepsilon_{m+1}\cdot\sqrt{\log|\mathfrak{N}_{m+1}|},

(F.4)

where the second line follows from the fact that
|\mathfrak{N}_{m+1}|\geq|\mathfrak{N}_{m}|
. Taking (
F.2
) and (
F.1
) into (
F.1
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}(\mathcal{F}_{j})

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon_{M}+\frac{6}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\sum_{m=1}^{M-1}\varepsilon_{m+1}\cdot\sqrt{\log|\mathfrak{N}_{m+1}|}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon_{M}+\frac{12}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\sum_{m=1}^{M}(\varepsilon_{m}-\varepsilon_{m+1})\cdot\sqrt{\log|\mathfrak{N}_{m}|}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon_{M}+\frac{12}{\sqrt{n}}\int_{\varepsilon_{M+1}}^{1/2}\sqrt{\log N\bigl(\mathfrak{I}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}(\mathcal{F}_{j}),\varepsilon,\|\cdot\|_{r,\infty}\bigr)}{\mathrm{d}}\varepsilon

\displaystyle\leq 4\xi+\frac{12}{\sqrt{n}}\int_{\xi}^{1/2}\sqrt{\log N\bigl(\mathfrak{I}_{\mathcal{D}_{n}}(\mathcal{F}_{j}),\varepsilon,\|\cdot\|_{r,\infty}\bigr)}{\mathrm{d}}\varepsilon,

where the last inequality holds for any
0<\xi<1
and the smallest
M
such that
\xi\leq\varepsilon_{M+1}
, which implies that
\varepsilon_{M}=2\varepsilon_{M+1}<4\xi
. Therefore, we conclude the proof of Lemma
C.8
.
∎

F.1.1
Proof of Matrix Ball Covering Lemma
C.10

Proof of Lemma
C.10
.

Let
M^{\top}=(m_{1},\dots,m_{d_{1}})\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{2}\times d_{1}}
, where
m_{j}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}}
with
j\in[d_{2}]
. We define the vectorization of the matrix
M\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}\times d_{2}}
as
{\mathrm{vec}}(M)=(m_{j}^{\top})^{\top}_{j\in[d_{2}]}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}d_{2}}
. We define the sectional norm for the vector
{\mathrm{vec}}(M)\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}d_{2}}
as follows,

\displaystyle\bigl\|{\mathrm{vec}}(M)\bigr\|_{r(d_{2}),s(d_{1})}=\|M^{\top}\|_{r,s},

which can be verified to be a proper norm. In Lemma
G.1
, setting

\displaystyle\mathbb{B}=\mathbb{B}_{*}=\bigl\{m\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}d_{2}}:\|m\|_{r(d_{2}),s(d_{1})}\leq 1\bigr\}=\bigl\{M^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{2}\times d_{1}}:\|M^{\top}\|_{r,s}\leq 1\bigr\},

and
\|\cdot\|=\|\cdot\|_{*}=\|\cdot\|_{r(d_{2}),s(d_{1})}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\log N\Bigl(\bigl\{M^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{2}\times d_{1}}:\|M^{\top}\|_{r,s}\leq R^{\mathrm{m}}\bigr\},\varepsilon,\|\cdot\|_{r,s}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad=\log N\Bigl(\bigl\{m\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}d_{2}}:\|m\|_{r(d_{2}),s(d_{1})}\leq 1\bigr\},\varepsilon/R^{M},\|\cdot\|_{r,s}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{\text{vol}(2R_{M}/\varepsilon\cdot\mathbb{B}_{*}+\mathbb{B})}{\text{vol}(\mathbb{B})}=d_{1}d_{2}\cdot\log\biggl(1+\frac{2R_{M}}{\varepsilon}\biggr).

Therefore, we conclude the proof of Lemma
C.10
.
∎

F.2
Lipschitz Continuity of Multihead Attention

Lemma F.1

(Lipschitz Continuous Softmax)
.

Let
(r,s)
be a conjugate pair. Under Assumption
C.1
, it holds for any
q,\widehat{q}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathrm{p}}}
and
K=(k^{\ell})_{\ell\in[L]}^{\top},\widehat{K}=(\widehat{k}^{\ell})_{\ell\in[L]}^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{L\times d_{\mathrm{p}}}
that

\displaystyle\Bigl\|\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(K,q)\bigr)-\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(\widehat{K},q)\bigr)\Bigr\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\bigl(\|q\|_{r}+\|K^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}\bigr)\cdot\|K^{\top}-\widehat{K}^{\top}\|_{r,\infty},

(F.5)

\displaystyle\Bigl\|\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(K,q)\bigr)-\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(K,\widehat{q})\bigr)\Bigr\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\bigl(\|q\|_{r}+\|K^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}\bigr)\cdot\|q-\widehat{q}\|_{r}.

(F.6)

Proof.

Let
P=\text{diag}(p)-pp^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{L\times L}
with
p=(p_{\ell})_{\ell\in[L]}=\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(K,q)\bigr)\in\mathbb{R}^{L}
. We have

\displaystyle p_{\ell}\propto\exp\bigl\{-\|q-k^{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}/2\sigma^{2}\bigr\}.

We define
g_{\ell}=-\|q-k^{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}/2\sigma^{2}
and
g=(g_{\ell})_{\ell\in[L]}^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{L}
. Let the Jacobian of
p\in\mathbb{R}^{L}
with respect to
k^{\ell}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{\mathrm{p}}}
be
J_{\ell}\in\mathbb{R}^{L\times d_{\mathrm{p}}}
. We have

\displaystyle J_{\ell}=\frac{\partial p}{\partial k^{\ell}}=\frac{\partial p}{\partial g}\cdot\frac{\partial g}{\partial k^{\ell}}=P\frac{\partial g}{\partial k^{\ell}},

where
\partial g/\partial k^{\ell}=(e_{\ell}q^{\top}-E_{\ell,\ell}K)/\sigma^{2}
. Here
E_{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}\in\mathbb{R}^{L\times L}
is the unit matrix whose
(\ell,\ell^{\prime})
-th entry is one and all other entries are zero. Note that

\displaystyle\biggl\|\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}J_{\ell}\Delta_{\ell}\biggr\|_{1}\leq\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\|J_{\ell}\Delta_{\ell}\|_{1}\leq\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\|J_{\ell}\|_{r\to 1}\cdot\|\Delta_{\ell}\|_{r}\leq\|\Delta\|_{r,\infty}\cdot\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\|J_{\ell}\|_{r\to 1},

where
\Delta=(\Delta_{\ell}^{\top})_{\ell\in[L]}
. Thus, the Lipschitz continuity constant of
\mathtt{softmax}(q,K)
is bounded by
\sum_{\ell=1}^{L}\|J_{\ell}\|_{r\to 1}
. Let
e_{\ell}\in\mathbb{R}^{L}
be the
\ell
-th one-hot vector with
\ell\in[L]
. For any
\ell\in[L]
, we have

\displaystyle\|J_{\ell}\|_{r\to 1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d_{\mathrm{p}}^{1-1/r}}{\sigma^{2}}\cdot\bigl\|P(e_{\ell}q^{\top}-E_{\ell,\ell}K)\bigr\|_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{d_{\mathrm{p}}^{1/s}}{\sigma^{2}}\cdot p_{\ell}\cdot\bigl\|(e_{\ell}-p)(q-k^{\ell})^{\top}\bigr\|_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{d_{\mathrm{p}}^{1/s}p_{\ell}}{\sigma^{2}}\cdot\|e_{\ell}-p\|_{s}\cdot\|q-k^{\ell}\|_{r},

(F.7)

where the equalities follow from
1/r+1/s=1
.
Summing up (
F.2
) for all
\ell\in[L]
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in[L]}\|J_{\ell}\|_{r\to 1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d_{\mathrm{p}}^{1/s}}{\sigma^{2}}\cdot\sum_{\ell\in[L]}p_{\ell}\cdot\|e_{\ell}-p\|_{s}\cdot\|q-k^{\ell}\|_{r}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d_{\mathrm{p}}^{1/s}}{\sigma^{2}}\cdot\bigl(\|q\|_{r}+\|K^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}\bigr)\cdot\sum_{\ell\in[L]}p_{\ell}\cdot\|e_{\ell}-p\|_{s}.

(F.8)

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in[L]}p_{\ell}\cdot\|e_{\ell}-p\|_{s}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell\in[L]}\biggl\{p_{\ell}\cdot\biggl[\sum_{\ell^{\prime}\neq l}p_{\ell^{\prime}}^{s}+(1-p_{\ell})^{s}\biggr]^{1/s}\biggr\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\ell\in[L]}p_{\ell}\cdot\bigl[2(1-p_{\ell})^{s}\bigr]^{1/s}\leq 2^{1/s}.

(F.9)

Combining (
F.2
) and (
F.2
), we have for
\sigma=(2d_{\mathrm{p}})^{1/2s}
that

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in[L]}\|J_{\ell}\|_{r\to 1}\leq\frac{(2d_{\mathrm{p}})^{1/s}}{\sigma^{2}}\cdot\bigl(\|q\|_{r}+\|K^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}\bigr).

Thus,
\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(K,q)\bigr)
is
(\|q\|_{r}+\|K^{\top}\|_{r,\infty})
-Lipschitz in
K^{\top}
with respect to
\|\cdot\|_{r,\infty}
, which concludes the proof of (
F.5
). Since
\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(q,k)=\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(k,q)
, we also have (
F.6
) by the same arguments for (
F.5
). Therefore, we conclude the proof of Lemma
F.1
.
∎

F.2.1
Proof of Lemma
C.12

Proof.

For notational simplicity, we write

\displaystyle{\mathtt{head}}_{i}=\mathtt{attn}_{\mathtt{SM}}(XW^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i},XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},XW^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i}),\quad\widehat{{\mathtt{head}}}_{i}=\mathtt{attn}_{\mathtt{SM}}(X\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i},X\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},X\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i}).

By the definition of sequence-to-sequence multihehead attention in (
C.3
), we have

\displaystyle\bigl\|{\mathtt{mha}}(X;W)^{\top}-{\mathtt{mha}}(X;\widehat{W})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\biggl\|\sum_{i=1}^{h}{\mathtt{head}}^{\top}_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{h}\widehat{{\mathtt{head}}}^{\top}_{i}\biggr\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{h}\bigl\|({\mathtt{head}}_{i}-\widehat{{\mathtt{head}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}.

(F.10)

Also, we have

\displaystyle\bigl\|({\mathtt{head}}_{i}-\widehat{{\mathtt{head}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}

(F.11)

\displaystyle\quad=\bigl\|\mathtt{attn}_{\mathtt{SM}}(XW^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i},XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},XW^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}-\mathtt{attn}_{\mathtt{SM}}(X\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i},X\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},X\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad=\biggl\|\Bigl((XW^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)\Bigr)_{\ell\in[L]}-\Bigl((X\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(X\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)\Bigr)_{\ell\in[L]}\biggr\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad=\max_{\ell\in[L]}\Bigl\|(XW^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)-(X\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(X\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)\Bigr\|_{r}.

Note that

\displaystyle\|x^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i}\|_{r}+\bigl\|(XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\bigl\|(XW^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}+\bigl\|(XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}

(F.12)

\displaystyle\leq\Bigl(\bigl\|(W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r}+\bigl\|(W^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r}\Bigr)\cdot\|X^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}\leq(\omega^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i}+\omega^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i})\cdot R.

Then for any
\ell\in[L]
, we have

\displaystyle\Bigl\|(XW^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)-(X\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(X\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)\Bigr\|_{r}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\Bigl\|\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(X\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)^{\top}X(W^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i}-\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})\Bigr\|_{r}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad+\biggl\|\Bigl(\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)-\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(X\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)\Bigr)^{\top}X{W}^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i}\biggr\|_{r}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\bigl\|(W^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i}-\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,s}\cdot\|X^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad+\Bigl\|\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)-\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(X\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}\widehat{W}^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)\Bigr\|_{1}\cdot\bigl\|({W}^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r}\cdot\|X^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq R\cdot\varepsilon_{i}^{\mathrm{v}}+\Bigl(\|x^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i}\|_{r}+\bigl\|(XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}\Bigr)\cdot\omega^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i}R^{2}\cdot(\varepsilon_{i}^{\mathrm{q}}+\varepsilon_{i}^{\mathrm{k}})

\displaystyle\quad\leq R\cdot\varepsilon_{i}^{\mathrm{v}}+(\omega^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i}+\omega^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i})\omega^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i}\cdot R^{3}\cdot(\varepsilon_{i}^{\mathrm{q}}+\varepsilon_{i}^{\mathrm{k}}),

(F.13)

where the third inequality follows from Lemma
G.3
, the fourth inequality follows from Lemma
F.1
, and the last inequality follows from (
F.12
). Taking (
F.2.1
) into (
F.11
), we have

\displaystyle\bigl\|({\mathtt{head}}_{i}-\widehat{{\mathtt{head}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}\leq R\cdot\varepsilon_{i}^{\mathrm{v}}+(\omega^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i}+\omega^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i})\omega^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i}\cdot R^{3}\cdot(\varepsilon_{i}^{\mathrm{q}}+\varepsilon_{i}^{\mathrm{k}}).

(F.14)

Taking (
F.14
) into (
F.2.1
), we obtain

\displaystyle\bigl\|{\mathtt{mha}}(X;W)^{\top}-{\mathtt{mha}}(X;\widehat{W})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}\leq R\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{h}\varepsilon_{i}^{\mathrm{v}}+R^{3}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{h}(\omega^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i}+\omega^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i})\cdot(\varepsilon_{i}^{\mathrm{q}}+\varepsilon_{i}^{\mathrm{k}}).

Therefore, we conclude the proof of Lemma
C.12
.
∎

F.2.2
Proof of Lemma
C.14

Proof.

In this proof, with a slight abuse of notations, we write

\displaystyle{\mathtt{head}}_{i}=\mathtt{attn}_{\mathtt{SM}}(XW^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i},XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},XW^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i}),\quad\widehat{{\mathtt{head}}}_{i}=\mathtt{attn}_{\mathtt{SM}}(\widehat{X}{W}^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i},\widehat{X}{W}^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},\widehat{X}{W}^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i}).

Similar to (
F.2.1
), we have

\displaystyle\bigl\|{\mathtt{mha}}(X;W)^{\top}-{\mathtt{mha}}(\widehat{X};W)^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{h}\bigl\|({\mathtt{head}}_{i}-\widehat{{\mathtt{head}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}.

For any fixed
\ell\in[L]
, we have

\displaystyle\bigl\|({\mathtt{head}}_{i}-\widehat{{\mathtt{head}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad=\biggl\|\Bigl((XW^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)-(\widehat{X}W^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(\widehat{X}W^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},\widehat{x}^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)\Bigr)_{\ell\in[L]}\biggr\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad=\max_{\ell\in[L]}\Bigl\|(XW^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)-(\widehat{X}W^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(\widehat{X}W^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},\widehat{x}^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)\Bigr\|_{r}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\max_{\ell\in[L]}\Bigl\|\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)\Bigr\|_{1}\cdot\bigl\|(W^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r}\cdot\|X^{\top}-\widehat{X}^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad+\max_{\ell\in[L]}\Bigl\|\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)-\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(\widehat{X}W^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},\widehat{x}^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)\Bigr\|_{1}\cdot\bigl\|({W}^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r}\cdot\|X^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\bigl\|(W^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r}\cdot\|X^{\top}-\widehat{X}^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad+\max_{\ell\in[L]}\bigl\|(W^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r}\cdot R^{2}\cdot(\omega^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i}+\omega^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i})\cdot\Bigl(\|x^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i}\|_{r}+\bigl\|(XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\omega^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i}\cdot\bigl[1+R^{2}\cdot(\omega^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i}+\omega^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i})^{2}\bigr]\cdot\|X^{\top}-\widehat{X}^{\top}\|_{r,\infty},

(F.15)

where the second inequality follows from Lemma
F.1
and (
F.12
), the third inequality follows from Lemma
F.1
, and the last inequality follows from (
F.12
). Summing up (
F.2.2
), we obtain

\displaystyle\bigl\|{\mathtt{mha}}(X;W)^{\top}-{\mathtt{mha}}(\widehat{X};W)^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\biggl\{\sum_{i=1}^{h}\omega^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i}\cdot\bigl[1+R^{2}\cdot(\omega^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i}+\omega^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i})^{2}\bigr]\biggr\}\cdot\|X^{\top}-\widehat{X}^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle=\biggl[\sum_{i=1}^{h}\omega^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i}+R^{2}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{h}(\omega^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i}+\omega^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i})^{2}\omega^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i}\biggr]\cdot\|X^{\top}-\widehat{X}^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}.

Therefore, we conclude the proof of Lemma
C.14
.
∎

Appendix G
Auxiliary Lemmas

Lemma G.1

(Volume Ratios and Metric Entropy,
Wainwright 2019
)
.

Consider a pair of norms
\|\cdot\|
and
\|\cdot\|_{*}
on
\mathbb{R}^{d}
, and let
\mathbb{B}
and
\mathbb{B}_{*}
be their corresponding unit balls. Then the
\varepsilon
-covering number of
\mathbb{B}_{*}
in the
\|\cdot\|
-norm obeys the bounds

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-d}\cdot\frac{\text{vol}(\mathbb{B}_{*})}{\text{vol}(\mathbb{B})}\leq N\bigl(\mathbb{B}_{*},\varepsilon,\|\cdot\|\bigr)\leq\frac{\text{vol}(2/\varepsilon\cdot\mathbb{B}_{*}+\mathbb{B})}{\text{vol}(\mathbb{B})}.

Lemma G.2

(
Caponnetto and De Vito 2007
)
.

Let
(\Omega,\nu)
be a probability space and
\xi
be a random variable on
\Omega
taking value in a real separable Hilbert space
\mathcal{H}
. We assume that there exists constants
B,\sigma>0
such that

\displaystyle\bigl\|\xi(w)\bigr\|_{\mathcal{H}}\leq B/2,\ \mathrm{a.s.},\quad\mathbb{E}\bigl[\|\xi\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\bigr]\leq\sigma^{2}.

Then, it holds with probability at least
1-\delta
that

\displaystyle\biggl\|L^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{L}\xi(\omega_{i})-\mathbb{E}[\xi]\biggr\|\leq 2\biggl(\frac{B}{L}+\frac{\sigma}{\sqrt{L}}\biggr)\log\frac{2}{\delta}.

Lemma G.3
.

Let
(r,s)
be a conjugate pair. For any
M\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}\times d_{2}}
,
u\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{2}\times 1}
, and
U\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{2}\times d_{3}}
, we have

\displaystyle\|Mu\|_{r}

\displaystyle\leq\|M\|_{r,\infty}\cdot\|u\|_{1},

\displaystyle\|MU\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|M\|_{r,s}\cdot\|U\|_{r,\infty},

\displaystyle\|MU\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|M\|_{r}\cdot\|U\|_{r,\infty}.

Proof.

Let
M=(m_{i})_{i\in[d_{2}]}
and
b=(b_{i})_{i\in[d_{2}]}^{\top}
. We have

\displaystyle\|Mu\|_{r}=\biggl\|\sum_{j=1}^{d_{2}}u_{j}\cdot m_{j}\biggr\|_{r}\leq\biggl(\sum_{j=1}^{d_{2}}|u_{j}|\biggr)\cdot\max_{j\in[d_{2}]}\|m_{j}\|_{r}=\|M\|_{r,\infty}\cdot\|u\|_{1}.

Also, we have

\displaystyle\|Mu\|_{r}=\biggl\|\sum_{j=1}^{d_{2}}u_{j}\cdot m_{j}\biggr\|_{r}\leq\biggl(\sum_{j=1}^{d_{2}}|u_{j}|\cdot\|m_{j}\|_{r}\biggr)\leq\|M\|_{r,s}\cdot\|u\|_{r}.

As a consequence, with
U=(u_{i})_{i\in[d_{3}]}
, we have

\displaystyle\|MU\|_{r,\infty}=\max_{j\in[d_{3}]}\|Mu_{j}\|_{r}\leq\max_{j\in[d_{3}]}\|M\|_{r,s}\cdot\|u_{j}\|_{r}=\|M\|_{r,s}\cdot\|U\|_{r,\infty}.

On the other hand, by the definition of the matrix operator norm, we obtain

\displaystyle\|MU\|_{r,\infty}=\max_{j\in[d_{3}]}\|Mu_{j}\|_{r}\leq\max_{j\in[d_{3}]}\|M\|_{r}\cdot\|u_{j}\|_{r}=\|M\|_{r}\cdot\|U\|_{r,\infty}.

Therefore, we conclude the proof of Lemma
G.3
.
∎

Lemma G.4

(Covering Coefficient Bounds)
.

We have for all
t=0,\dots,T-1
that

\displaystyle 1+\rho(\mathfrak{W}^{(t)})

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{\omega}^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}+(\overline{\omega}^{{\mathrm{q}}{\mathrm{k}},(t)})^{2}\omega^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}\cdot(R^{(t)})^{2}=\widetilde{\rho}^{(t)},

\displaystyle R_{\mathtt{mha}}(\mathfrak{W}^{(t)})

\displaystyle\leq R^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}+\overline{\omega}^{{\mathrm{q}}{\mathrm{k}},(t)}{R}^{{\mathrm{q}}{\mathrm{k}},(t)}\cdot(R^{(t)})^{2}=R^{(t)}_{\mathtt{mha}},

where
\widetilde{\rho}^{(t)}
and
R^{(t)}_{\mathtt{mha}}
are defined in (
C.20
).

Proof.

By the definition of
R_{\mathtt{mha}}(\mathfrak{W})
in (
C.32
), we have

\displaystyle R_{\mathtt{mha}}(\mathfrak{W}^{(t)})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{h}R^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}_{i}+(R^{{\mathrm{x}},(t)})^{2}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{h}(\omega_{i}^{{\mathrm{q}},(t)}+\omega_{i}^{{\mathrm{k}},(t)})(R_{i}^{{\mathrm{q}},(t)}+R_{i}^{{\mathrm{k}},(t)})

\displaystyle\leq R^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}+(R^{(t)})^{2}\cdot\max_{i\in[h]}\{\omega_{i}^{{\mathrm{q}},(t)}+\omega_{i}^{{\mathrm{k}},(t)}\}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{h}(R_{i}^{{\mathrm{q}},(t)}+R_{i}^{{\mathrm{k}},(t)})

\displaystyle=R^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}+\overline{\omega}^{{\mathrm{q}}{\mathrm{k}},(t)}{R}^{{\mathrm{q}}{\mathrm{k}},(t)}\cdot(R^{(t)})^{2}=R^{(t)}_{\mathtt{mha}}.

Also, by the definition of
\rho(\mathfrak{W})
in (
C.34
), we have

\displaystyle 1+{\rho}(\mathfrak{W}^{(t)})

\displaystyle=1+\sum_{i=1}^{h}\omega^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}_{i}+(R^{{\mathrm{x}},(t)})^{2}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{h}(\omega^{{\mathrm{q}},(t)}_{i}+\omega^{{\mathrm{k}},(t)}_{i})^{2}\omega^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}_{i}

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{\omega}^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}+(R^{(t)})^{2}\cdot\max_{i\in[h]}\{\omega_{i}^{{\mathrm{q}},(t)}+\omega_{i}^{{\mathrm{k}},(t)}\}^{2}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{h}\omega^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}_{i}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\omega}^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}+(\overline{\omega}^{{\mathrm{q}}{\mathrm{k}},(t)})^{2}\omega^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}\cdot(R^{(t)})^{2}=\widetilde{\rho}^{(t)}.

Therefore, we conclude the proof of Lemma
G.4
.
∎

Lemma G.5

(Simplified Covering Coefficient)
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\sqrt{\log(1+R^{(T)}R_{\mathtt{trans}})}=O\Bigl(T\sqrt{\log(1+\gamma)}+\sqrt{T}\sqrt{\log(1+\zeta R^{(0)})}+\sqrt{\log(1+\kappa/\zeta)}\Bigr),

where
\gamma
,
\zeta
, and
\kappa
are defined in (
C.13
). Here
R^{(t)}
and
R_{\mathtt{trans}}
are defined in (
C.18
) and (
C.19
), respectively.

Proof.

By the definitions of
\kappa^{(t)}
and
\zeta^{(t)}
in (
C.12
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{R^{(t)}_{\mathtt{mha}}}{\widetilde{\rho}^{(t)}}

\displaystyle=\frac{R^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}+\overline{\omega}^{{\mathrm{q}}{\mathrm{k}},(t)}{R}^{{\mathrm{q}}{\mathrm{k}},(t)}\cdot(R^{(t)})^{2}}{\widetilde{\omega}^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}+(\overline{\omega}^{{\mathrm{q}}{\mathrm{k}},(t)})^{2}\omega^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}\cdot(R^{(t)})^{2}}\leq\max\biggl\{\frac{R^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}}{\widetilde{\omega}^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}},\frac{{R}^{{\mathrm{q}}{\mathrm{k}},(t)}}{\overline{\omega}^{{\mathrm{q}}{\mathrm{k}},(t)}\omega^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}}\biggr\}\leq\kappa^{(t)},

\displaystyle\frac{\widetilde{\rho}^{(t)}}{\widetilde{\omega}^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}}

\displaystyle=1+\frac{(\overline{\omega}^{{\mathrm{q}}{\mathrm{k}},(t)})^{2}\omega^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}}{\widetilde{\omega}^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}}\cdot(R^{(t)})^{2}=1+\zeta^{(t)}\cdot(R^{(t)})^{2},

which implies

\displaystyle{R}_{\mathtt{trans}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=0}^{T-2}\biggl(\frac{R^{(t)}_{\mathtt{mha}}}{\widetilde{\rho}^{(t)}}\cdot\prod_{\tau=t+1}^{T-1}\frac{\widetilde{\rho}^{(\tau)}}{\widetilde{\omega}^{{\mathrm{v}},(\tau)}}\biggr)+\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\biggl(\frac{\alpha^{{\mathrm{x}},(t)}R^{\sigma,(t)}+\alpha^{\sigma,(t)}R^{{\mathrm{x}},(t)}}{\widetilde{\alpha}^{(t)}}\cdot\prod_{\tau=t+1}^{T-1}\frac{\widetilde{\rho}^{(\tau)}}{\widetilde{\omega}^{{\mathrm{v}},(\tau)}}\biggr)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\biggl\{(\kappa^{(t)}+\kappa^{(t)})\cdot\prod_{\tau=t+1}^{T-1}\bigl[1+\zeta^{(t)}\cdot(R^{(t)})^{2}\bigr]\biggr\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\kappa\cdot\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\biggl\{\prod_{\tau=t+1}^{T-1}\bigl[1+\zeta\cdot(R^{(t)})^{2}\bigr]\biggr\},

where the last line follows from the definition of
\kappa
and
\zeta
in (
C.13
). By the definition of
R^{(t)}
in (
C.18
) and the definition of
\gamma
in (
C.13
), we have

\displaystyle R^{(t)}=R^{(0)}\cdot\prod_{\tau=0}^{t-1}\widetilde{\omega}^{{\mathrm{v}},(\tau)}\widetilde{\alpha}^{(\tau)}\leq R^{(0)}\cdot(1+\gamma)^{2t}.

As a consequence, we obtain

\displaystyle R^{(T)}R_{\mathtt{trans}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\kappa R^{(0)}\cdot(1+\gamma)^{2T}\cdot\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\prod_{\tau=t+1}^{T-1}\bigl[1+\zeta R^{(0)}\cdot(1+\gamma)^{4\tau}\bigr]

\displaystyle\leq 2\kappa R^{(0)}\cdot(1+\gamma)^{2T}\cdot\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\bigl[1+\zeta R^{(0)}\cdot(1+\gamma)^{4T}\bigr]^{T-t+2}

\displaystyle=2\kappa\cdot\bigl[1+\zeta R^{(0)}\cdot(1+\gamma)^{4T}\bigr]^{3}\cdot\frac{\bigl[1+\zeta R^{(0)}\cdot(1+\gamma)^{4T}\bigr]^{T}-1}{\zeta\cdot(1+\gamma)^{2T}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\kappa}{\zeta}\cdot\bigl[1+\zeta R^{(0)}\cdot(1+\gamma)^{4T}\bigr]^{T+3}.

Thus, using
\sqrt{a+b}\leq\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b}
and
\log(1+ab)\leq\log(1+a)+\log(1+b)
, we further obtain

\displaystyle\sqrt{\log(1+R^{(T)}R_{\mathtt{trans}})}

\displaystyle=O\Bigl(\sqrt{T^{2}\log(1+\gamma)+T\log(1+\zeta R^{(0)})+\log(1+\kappa/\zeta)}\Bigr)

\displaystyle=O\Bigl(T\sqrt{\log(1+\gamma)}+\sqrt{T}\sqrt{\log(1+\zeta R^{(0)})}+\sqrt{\log(1+\kappa/\zeta)}\Bigr).

Therefore, we conclude the proof of Lemma
G.5
.
∎

Lemma G.6

(Inter-Layer Magnitude)
.

Under Assumptions
5.2
and
C.3
, it holds that

\displaystyle\bigl\|(X^{(t)})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}\leq\widetilde{\alpha}^{(t)}R^{(t)},\qquad\bigl\|(X_{\star}^{(t)})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}\leq R^{(t)},

where
R^{(t)}
is defined in (
C.18
).

Proof.

Setting
\widehat{X}=0^{L\times d}
in (
F.2.1
), we have

\displaystyle\bigl\|{\mathtt{mha}}(X;W)^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{h}\|{\mathtt{head}}_{i}^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}.

We have for all
i\in[h]
that

\displaystyle\|{{\mathtt{head}}}_{i}^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle=\biggl\|\Bigl((X{W}^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)\Bigr)_{\ell\in[L]}\biggr\|_{r,\infty}

\displaystyle=\max_{l\in[L]}\Bigl\|(X{W}^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)\Bigr\|_{r}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{l\in[L]}\Bigl\|\mathtt{norm}_{\mathtt{SM}}\bigl(\mathfrak{K}_{\mathtt{RBF}}(XW^{{\mathrm{k}}}_{i},x^{\ell}W^{{\mathrm{q}}}_{i})\bigr)\Bigr\|_{1}\cdot\bigl\|(W^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r}\cdot\|X^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}\leq\omega^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i}\cdot\|X^{\top}\|_{r,\infty},

which implies that

\displaystyle\bigl\|{\mathtt{mha}}(X;W)^{\top}+X^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}\leq\|X^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}+\biggl(\sum_{i=1}^{h}\omega^{{\mathrm{v}}}_{i}\biggr)\cdot\|X^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}=(1+\omega^{{\mathrm{v}}})\cdot\|X^{\top}\|_{r,\infty}.

As a consequence, we have

\displaystyle\bigl\|(X_{\star}^{(t)})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}\leq(1+\omega^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)})\cdot\bigl\|(X^{(t)})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}=\widetilde{\omega}^{{\mathrm{v}},(t)}\cdot\bigl\|(X^{(t)})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}.

(G.1)

On the other hand, setting
\widehat{X}_{\star}^{(t)}=0^{L\times d}
in (
C.5.2
), we have

\displaystyle\bigl\|(X^{(t+1)})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}=\bigl\|{\mathtt{ffn}}({X}_{\star}^{(t)};A^{(t)})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}\leq\widetilde{\alpha}^{(t)}\cdot\bigl\|({X}_{\star}^{(t)})^{\top}\bigr\|_{r,\infty}.

(G.2)

Recursively applying (
G.1
) and (
G.2
), we conclude the proof of Lemma
G.6
.
∎
